# pf2e's conditions and effects

Generated by `npm run index:pf2e` from pf2e 8.4.1 — do not edit by hand. Every condition and effect
the system ships: **2948** items, cut into 12 parts, each with its own issue and clause tracker in
`Docs/clauses/pf2e-effects/`.

| Part | What | Items |
| :-- | :-- | --: |
| 1 | Conditions, and the small packs | 172 |
| 2 | Spell effects, 1 of 2 | 271 |
| 3 | Spell effects, 2 of 2 | 270 |
| 4 | Feat effects, 1 of 4 | 212 |
| 5 | Feat effects, 2 of 4 | 212 |

| 7 | Feat effects, 4 of 4 | 210 |
| 8 | Equipment effects, 1 of 3 | 240 |

| 10 | Equipment effects, 3 of 3 | 240 |
